Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the chromatography framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 48. The resulting operator achieves bound O(394/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by chromatography generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 48, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 394. QED.
